useActivitiesStore = defineStore('activities', () => {\n    12→  const householdStore = useHouseholdStore()\n    13→  const toast = useToast()\n    14→  const { trackActivityLogged, if (petsStore.selectedPetId === 'all') {\n    27→      return activities.value\n    28→    }\n    29→\n    30→    return activities.value.filter(a => a.petId === petsStore.selectedPetId)\n    31→  })\n    32→\n    33→  const sortedActivities = computed(() => {\n    34→    return [...filteredActivities.value].sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)\n    35→  })\n    36→\n    37→  const todayActivities = computed(() => {\n    38→    const today = new Date()\n    39→    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)\n    40→    const todayTimestamp = today.getTime()\n    41→\n    42→    return sortedActivities.value.filter(a => a.timestamp >= todayTimestamp)\n    43→  })\n    44→\n    45→  const stats = computed(() => {\n    46→    const today = todayActivities.value\n    47→    return {\n    48→      poop: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Poop').length,\n    49→      pee: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Pee').length,\n    50→      food: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Food').length,\n    51→      sleep: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Sleep').length,\n    52→      meds: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Meds').length,\n    53→      walk: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Walk').length,\n    54→      vetVisit: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Vet Visit').length,\n    55→      vaccination: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Vaccination').length,\n    56→      weightCheck: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Weight Check').length,\n    57→      total: today.length\n    58→    }\n    59→  })\n    60→\n    61→  // Actions\n    62→  function startListener() {\n    63→    if (!householdStore.householdId) {\n    64→      console.error('Cannot start listener: No household ID')\n    65→      return\n    66→    }\n    67→\n    68→    const activitiesRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities`)\n    69→\n    70→    listener.value = onValue(activitiesRef, (snapshot) => {\n    71→      const data = snapshot.val()\n    72→\n    73→      if (data) {\n    74→        activities.value = Object.entries(data).map(([id, activity]) => ({\n    75→          id,\n    76→          ...activity\n    77→        }))\n    78→      } else {\n    79→        activities.value = []\n    80→      }\n    81→    }, (error) => {\n    82→      console.error('Firebase listener error:', error)\n    83→      toast.error('Failed to sync activities')\n    84→    })\n    85→  }\n    86→\n    87→  function stopListener() {\n    88→    if (listener.value) {\n    89→      listener.value()\n    90→      listener.value = null\n    91→    }\n    92→  }\n    93→\n    94→  async function uploadPhoto(file) {\n    95→    try {\n    96→      const timestamp = Date.now()\n    97→      const filename = `${timestamp}-${file.name}`\n    98→      const photoRef = }\n   109→\n   110→  async function logActivity(type, emoji, notes = '', photoFile = null, medicalData = null) {\n   111→    if (!householdStore.householdId || !householdStore.memberName) {\n   112→ Require member selection\n   125→    if (!householdStore.currentMember) {\n   126→      toast.warning('Please select who is logging this activity')\n   127→      return false\n   128→    }\n   129→\n   130→    try {\n   131→      loading.value = true\n   132→\n   133→      // Upload photo if provided\n   134→      let photoUrl = null\n   135→      if (photoFile) {\n   136→        toast.info('Uploading photo...')\n   137→        photoUrl = await uploadPhoto(photoFile, type)\n   138→      }\n   139→\n   140→      const activity = {\n   141→        type,\n   142→        emoji,\n 'default' : petsStore.selectedPetId,\n   146→        notes\n   147→      }\n   148→\n   149→      // Only add photoUrl if it exists\n   150→      if (photoUrl) {\n   151→        activity.photoUrl = photoUrl\n   152→      }\n   153→\n   154→      // Add medical data if provided\n   155→      if (medicalData) {\n   156→        activity.medicalData = medicalData\n   157→      }\n   158→\n   159→      const activitiesRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities`)\n   160→      await push(activitiesRef, activity)\n   161→\n   162→      // Track analytics\n   163→      if (medicalData) {\n   164→        trackMedicalActivity(type)\n   165→      } else {\n   166→        trackActivityLogged(type, activity.petId, !!notes, !!photoUrl)\n   167→      }\n   168→\n   169→      toast.success(`${emoji} ${type} logged!`)\n   170→      return true\n   171→    } catch (error) {\n   172→      console.error('Error logging activity:', error)\n   173→\n   174→      // Note: Don't queue activities with photos or medical data for offline sync (too complex)\n   175→      if (!photoFile && !medicalData) {\n   176→        const activity = {\n   177→          type,\n   178→          emoji,\n   179→          timestamp: Date.now(),\n   180→          user: householdStore.currentMember,\n   181→          petId: petsStore.selectedPetId === 'all' ? 'default' : petsStore.selectedPetId,\n   182→          notes\n   183→        }\n   184→        offlineQueue.value.push(activity)\n   185→        toast.warning('Saved offline. Will sync when online.')\n   186→      } else {\n   187→        toast.error('Failed to log activity. Please try again.')\n   188→      }\n   189→\n   190→      return false\n   191→    } finally {\n   192→      loading.value = false\n   193→    }\n   194→  }\n   195→\n   196→  async function deleteActivity(activityId) {\n   197→    if (!householdStore.householdId) {\n   198→      toast.error('Please sign in first')\n   199→      return false\n   200→    }\n   201→\n   202→    try {\n   203→      const activityRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities/${activityId}`)\n   204→      await remove(activityRef)\n   205→      toast.success('Activity deleted')\n   206→      return true\n   207→    } catch (error) {\n   208→      console.error('Error deleting activity:', error)\n   209→      toast.error('Failed to delete activity')\n   210→      return false\n   211→    }\n   212→  }\n   213→\n   214→  async function updateActivity(activityId, updates) {\n   215→    if (!householdStore.householdId) {\n   216→      toast.error('Please sign in 222→      await update(activityRef, updates)\n   223→      toast.success('Activity updated')\n   224→      return true\n   225→    } catch (error) {\n   226→      console.error('Error updating activity:', error)\n   227→      toast.error('Failed to update activity')\n   228→      return false\n   229→    }\n   230→  }\n   231→\n   232→  async function syncOfflineQueue() {\n   233→    if (offlineQueue.value.length === 0) return\n   234→\n   235→    const queue = [...offlineQueue.value]\n   236→    offlineQueue.value = []\n   237→\n   238→    for (const activity of queue) {\n   239→      try {\n   240→        const activitiesRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities`)\n   241→        await push(activitiesRef, activity)\n   242→      } catch (error) {\n   243→        console.error('Failed to sync offline activity:', error)\n   244→        offlineQueue.value.push(activity)\n   245→      }\n   246→    }\n   247→\n   248→    if function loadOfflineQueue() {\n   255→    const saved = localStorage.getItem('offlineQueue')\n   256→    if (saved) {\n   257→      try {\n   258→        offlineQueue.value = JSON.parse(saved)\n activities,\n   273→    loading,\n   274→    offlineQueue,\n   275→\n   276→    // Computed\n   277→    sortedActivities,\n   278→    todayActivities,\n   279→    stats,\n   280→\n   281→    //     1→import { ref } from 'vue'\n     2→\n     3→// Global toast state (singleton pattern)\n     4→const toasts = ref([])\n     5→let toastId = 0\n     6→\n     7→export function useToast() {\n     8→  function show(message, type = 'info', duration = 3000) {\n     9→    const id = toastId++\n    10→    const toast = {\n    11→      id,\n    12→      message,\n    13→      type, // 'info', 'success', 'error', 'warning'\n    14→      duration\n    15→    }\n    16→\n    17→    toasts.value.push(toast)\n    18→\n    19→    // Auto-remove after duration\n    20→    if (duration > 0) {\n    21→      setTimeout(() => {\n    22→        remove(id)\n    23→      }, duration)\n    24→    }\n    25→\n    26→    return id\n    27→  }\n    28→\n    29→  function remove(id) {\n    30→    const index = toasts.value.findIndex(t => t.id === id)\n    31→    if (index > -1) {\n    32→      toasts.value.splice(index, 1)\n    33→    }\n    34→  }\n    35→\n    36→  function success(message, duration = 3000) {\n    37→    return show(message, 'success', duration)\n    38→  }\n    39→\n    40→  function error(message, duration = 5000) {\n    41→    return show(message, 'error', duration)\n    42→  }\n    43→\n    44→  function warning(message, duration = 4000) {\n    45→    return show(message, 'warning', duration)\n    46→  }\n    47→\n    48→  function info(message, duration = 3000) {\n    49→    return show(message, 'info', duration)\n    50→  }\n    51→\n    52→  return {\n    53→    toasts,\n    54→    show,\n    55→    remove,\n    56→    success,\n    57→    error,\n    58→    warning,\n    59→    info\n    60→  }\n    61→}\n    62→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Action type (created, joined, invited, etc.)\n    56→   * @param {Object} params - Additional parameters\n    57→   */\n    58→  const trackHouseholdAction = (action, params = {}) => {\n    59→    trackEvent('household_action', {\n    60→      action: action,\n    61→      ...params\n    62→    })\n    63→  }\n    64→\n    65→  /**\n    66→   * Track invite sent\n    67→   * @param {string} method -     1→import { jsPDF } from 'jspdf'\n     2→import { format } from 'date-fns'\n     3→\n     4→export function usePdfExport() {\n     5→  /**\n     6→   * Generate a medical history PDF for a pet\n     7→   * @param {Object} pet - Pet object with name, emoji, species\n     8→   * @param {Array} activities - All activities for the pet\n     9→   * @param {Object} household - Household info\n    10→   */\n    11→  function generateMedicalPdf(pet, activities, household) {\n    12→    try {\n    13→      // Create new PDF (portrait, mm, A4)\n    14→      const doc = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4')\n    15→      const pageWidth = doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth()\n    16→      const pageHeight = doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight()\n    17→      const margin = 20\n    18→      const contentWidth = pageWidth - (margin * 2)\n    19→      let yPos = margin\n    20→\n    21→      // Helper function to check if new page is needed\n    22→      const checkNewPage = (neededHeight) => {\n    23→        if (yPos + neededHeight > pageHeight - margin) {\n    24→          doc.addPage()\n    25→          yPos = margin\n    26→          return true\n    27→        }\n    28→        return false\n    29→      }\n    30→\n    31→      // Helper function to add footer\n    32→      const addFooter = () => {\n    33→        const pageNum = doc.internal.getCurrentPageInfo().pageNumber\n    34→        doc.setFontSize(8)\n    35→        doc.setTextColor(150)\n    36→        doc.text(\n    37→          `Generated by Tailr - Page ${pageNum}`,\n    38→          pageWidth / 2,\n    39→          pageHeight - 10,\n    40→          { align: 'center' }\n    41→        )\n    42→        doc.text(\n    43→          format(new Date(), 'MMM d, yyyy'),\n    44→          pageWidth - margin,\n    45→          pageHeight - 10,\n    46→          { align: 'right' }\n    47→        )\n    48→      }\n    49→\n    50→      // ===== HEADER =====\n    51→      doc.setFillColor(16, 185, 129) // Sage green\n    52→      doc.rect(0, 0, pageWidth, 40, 'F')\n    53→\n    54→      doc.setFontSize(24)\n    55→      doc.setTextColor(255, 255, 255)\n    56→      doc.text('🐾 Tailr', margin, yPos + 10)\n    57→\n    58→      doc.setFontSize(14)\n    59→      doc.text('Medical History Report', margin, yPos + 20)\n    60→\n    61→      yPos = 50\n    62→\n    63→      // ===== PET INFO =====\n    64→      doc.setFontSize(16)\n    65→      doc.setTextColor(17, 24, 39)\n    66→      doc.text(`${pet.emoji} ${pet.name}`, margin, yPos)\n    67→      yPos += 8\n    68→\n    69→      doc.setFontSize(10)\n    70→      doc.setTextColor(100)\n    71→      doc.text(`Species: ${pet.species || 'Unknown'}`, margin, yPos)\n    72→      yPos += 5\n    73→      if (pet.breed) {\n    74→        doc.text(`Breed: ${pet.breed}`, margin, yPos)\n    75→        yPos += 5\n    76→      }\n    77→      doc.text(`Household: ${household.memberName || 'Unknown'}`, margin, yPos)\n    78→      yPos += 5\n    79→      doc.text(`Report Date: ${format(new Date(), 'MMMM d, yyyy')}`, margin, yPos)\n    80→      yPos += 12\n    81→\n    82→      // Separator line\n    83→      doc.setDrawColor(200)\n    84→      doc.line(margin, yPos, pageWidth - margin, yPos)\n    85→      yPos += 10\n    86→\n    87→      // Filter medical activities\n    88→      const vetVisits = activities.filter(a => a.type === 'Vet Visit').sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)\n    89→      const vaccinations = activities.filter(a => a.type === 'Vaccination').sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)\n    90→      const weightChecks = activities.filter(a => a.type === 'Weight Check').sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)\n    91→\n    92→      // ===== VET VISITS SECTION =====\n    93→      if (vetVisits.length > 0) {\n    94→        checkNewPage(20)\n    95→        doc.setFontSize(14)\n    96→        doc.setTextColor(17, 24, 39)\n    97→        doc.text('🏥 Vet Visits', margin, yPos)\n    98→        yPos += 8\n    99→\n   100→        vetVisits.forEach((visit) => {\n   101→          const boxHeight = 20 + (visit.medicalData?.notes ? 10 : 0)\n   102→          checkNewPage(boxHeight + 5)\n   103→\n   104→          // Box for each visit\n   105→          doc.setFillColor(249, 250, 251)\n   106→          doc.roundedRect(margin, yPos, contentWidth, boxHeight, 3, 3, 'F')\n   107→\n   108→          doc.setFontSize(10)\n   109→          doc.setTextColor(17, 24, 39)\n   110→          doc.setFont(undefined, 'bold')\n   111→          doc.text(\n   112→            format(visit.timestamp, 'MMM d, yyyy'),\n   113→            margin + 5,\n   114→            yPos + 6\n   115→          )\n   116→\n   117→          doc.setFont(undefined, 'normal')\n   118→          doc.setTextColor(100)\n   119→\n   120→          if (visit.medicalData?.cost) {\n   121→            doc.text(\n   122→              `Cost: $${visit.medicalData.cost.toFixed(2)}`,\n   123→              pageWidth - margin - 5,\n   124→        })\n   136→\n   137→        yPos += 5\n   138→      }\n   139→\n   140→      // ===== VACCINATIONS SECTION =====\n   141→      if (vaccinations.length > 0) {\n   142→        checkNewPage(20)\n vaccinations.forEach((vacc) => {\n   149→          const boxHeight = 20 + (vacc.medicalData?.notes ? 10 : 0)\n   150→          checkNewPage(boxHeight + 5)\n   151→\n   152→ class=\"empty-hint\">Log weight checks to see trends over time</p>\n    27→    </div>\n    28→\n    29→    <div v-else class=\"chart-container\">\n    30→      <canvas ref=\"chartCanvas\"></canvas>\n <span\n    39→            class=\"stat-value\"\n    40→            :class=\"{\n    41→              'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400': weightChange > 0,\n    42→              'text-green-600 '+' : '' }}{{ weightChange }} {{ displayUnit }}\n    46→          </span>\n    47→        </div>\n    48→        <div class=\"stat-item\">\n    49→          <span class=\"stat-label\">Checks</span>\n    50→          <span class=\"stat-value\">{{ weightData.length }}</span>\n    51→        </div>\n    52→      </div>\n    53→    </div>\n    54→  </div>\n    55→</template>\n    56→\n    57→<script setup>\n    58→import { ref, computed, watch, onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue'\n    59→import { Chart, LineController, LineElement, PointElement, LinearScale, TimeScale, Title, Tooltip, Legend, Filler } from 'chart.js'\n    60→import 'chartjs-adapter-date-fns'\n    61→import { format } from 'date-fns'\n    62→\n    63→// Register Chart.js components\n    64→Chart.register(LineController, LineElement, PointElement, LinearScale, TimeScale, Title, Tooltip, Legend, Filler)\n    65→\n    66→const props = defineProps({\n    67→  activities: {\n    68→    type: Array,\n    69→    required: true\n    70→  }\n    71→})\n    72→\n    73→const chartCanvas = ref(null)\n    74→const chartInstance = ref(null)\n    75→const displayUnit = ref('lbs')\n    76→\n    77→// Extract and sort weight data\n    78→const weightData = computed(() => {\n    79→  const weights = props.activities\n    80→    .filter(a => a.type === 'Weight Check' && a.medicalData?.weight)\n    81→    .map(a => ({\n    82→      timestamp: a.timestamp,\n    83→      weight: a.medicalData.weight,\n    84→      unit: a.medicalData.unit || 'lbs',\n    85→      date: new Date(a.timestamp)\n    86→    }))\n    87→    .sort((a, b) => a.timestamp - b.timestamp)\n    88→\n    89→  return weights\n    90→})\n    91→\n    92→// Convert weight to display unit\n    93→function convertWeight(weight, fromUnit) {\n    94→  if (fromUnit === displayUnit.value) return weight\n    95→  if (displayUnit.value === 'kg') {\n    96→    return weight / 2.20462 // lbs to kg\n    97→  } else {\n    98→    return weight * 2.20462 // kg to lbs\n    99→  }\n   100→}\n   101→\n   102→// Chart data in display unit\n   103→const chartData = computed(() => {\n   104→  return weightData.value.map(w => ({\n   105→    x: w.timestamp,\n   106→    y: parseFloat(convertWeight(w.weight, w.unit).toFixed(1))\n   107→  }))\n   108→})\n   109→\n   110→// Latest weight\n   111→const latestWeight = computed(() => {\n   112→  if (weightData.value.length === 0) return 0\n   113→  const latest = weightData.value[weightData.value.length - 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1)' : 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2)',\n   138→    grid: dark ? 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)' : 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)',\n   139→    text: dark ? '#d1d5db' : '#374151'\n   140→  }\n   141→})\n   142→\n   143→// Create or update chart\n   144→function createChart() {\n   145→  if (!chartCanvas.value || chartData.value.length === 0) return\n   146→\n   147→  // Destroy existing chart\n   148→  if (chartInstance.value) {\n   149→    chartInstance.value.destroy()\n   150→  }\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware.     1→import { ref } from 'vue'\n     2→\n     3→// Global toast state (singleton pattern)\n     4→const toasts = ref([])\n     5→let toastId = 0\n     6→\n     7→export function useToast() {\n     8→  function show(message, type = 'info', duration = 3000) {\n     9→    const id = toastId++\n    10→    const toast = {\n    11→      id,\n    12→      message,\n    13→      type, // 'info', 'success', 'error', 'warning'\n    14→      duration\n    15→    }\n    16→\n    17→    toasts.value.push(toast)\n    18→\n    19→    // Auto-remove after duration\n    20→    if (duration > 0) {\n    21→      setTimeout(() => {\n    22→        remove(id)\n    23→      }, duration)\n    24→    }\n    25→\n    26→    return id\n    27→  }\n    28→\n    29→  function remove(id) {\n    30→    const index = toasts.value.findIndex(t => t.id === id)\n    31→    if (index > -1) {\n    32→      toasts.value.splice(index, 1)\n    33→    }\n    34→  }\n    35→\n    36→  function success(message, if (petsStore.selectedPetId === 'all') {\n    27→      return activities.value\n    28→    }\n    29→\n    30→    return activities.value.filter(a => a.petId === petsStore.selectedPetId)\n    31→  })\n    32→\n    33→  const sortedActivities = computed(() => {\n    34→    return [...filteredActivities.value].sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)\n    35→  })\n    36→\n    37→  const todayActivities = computed(() => {\n    38→    const today = new Date()\n    39→    today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)\n    40→    const todayTimestamp = today.getTime()\n    41→\n    42→    return sortedActivities.value.filter(a => a.timestamp >= todayTimestamp)\n    43→  })\n    44→\n    45→  const stats = computed(() => {\n    46→    const today = todayActivities.value\n    47→    return {\n    48→      poop: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Poop').length,\n    49→      pee: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Pee').length,\n    50→      food: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Food').length,\n    51→      sleep: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Sleep').length,\n    52→      meds: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Meds').length,\n    53→      walk: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Walk').length,\n    54→      vetVisit: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Vet Visit').length,\n    55→      vaccination: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Vaccination').length,\n    56→      weightCheck: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Weight Check').length,\n    57→      total: today.length\n    58→    }\n    59→  })\n    60→\n    61→  // Actions\n    62→  function startListener() {\n    63→    if (!householdStore.householdId) {\n    64→      console.error('Cannot start listener: No household ID')\n    65→      return\n    66→    }\n    67→\n    68→    const activitiesRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities`)\n    69→\n    70→    listener.value = onValue(activitiesRef, (snapshot) => {\n    71→      const data = snapshot.val()\n    72→\n    73→      if (data) {\n    74→        activities.value = Object.entries(data).map(([id, activity]) => ({\n    75→          id,\n    76→          ...activity\n    77→        }))\n    78→      } else {\n    79→        activities.value = []\n    80→      }\n    81→    }, (error) => {\n    82→      console.error('Firebase listener error:', error)\n    83→      toast.error('Failed to sync activities')\n    84→    })\n    85→  }\n    86→\n    87→  function stopListener() {\n    88→    if (listener.value) {\n    89→      listener.value()\n    90→      listener.value = null\n    91→    }\n    92→  }\n    93→\n    94→  async function uploadPhoto(file) {\n    95→    try {\n    96→      const timestamp = Date.now()\n    97→      const filename = `${timestamp}-${file.name}`\n    98→      const photoRef = }\n   109→\n   110→  async function logActivity(type, emoji, notes = '', photoFile = null, medicalData = null) {\n   111→    if (!householdStore.householdId || !householdStore.memberName) {\n   112→  130→    try {\n   131→      loading.value = true\n   132→\n   133→      // Upload photo if provided\n   134→      let photoUrl = null\n   135→      if (photoFile) {\n   136→        toast.info('Uploading photo...')\n   137→        photoUrl = await uploadPhoto(photoFile, type)\n   138→      }\n   139→\n   140→      const activity = {\n   141→        type,\n   142→        emoji,\n   143→        timestamp: Date.now(),\n   144→        user: householdStore.currentMember,\n   145→        petId: petsStore.selectedPetId === 'all' ? 'default' : petsStore.selectedPetId,\n   146→        notes\n   147→      }\n   148→\n   149→      // Only add photoUrl if it exists\n   150→      if (photoUrl) {\n   151→        activity.photoUrl = photoUrl\n   152→      }\n   153→\n   154→      // Add medical data if provided\n   155→      if (medicalData) {\n   156→        activity.medicalData = medicalData\n   157→      }\n   158→\n   159→      const activitiesRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities`)\n   160→      await push(activitiesRef, activity)\n   161→\n   162→      // Track analytics\n   163→      if (medicalData) {\n   164→        trackMedicalActivity(type)\n   165→      } else {\n   166→        trackActivityLogged(type, activity.petId, !!notes, !!photoUrl)\n   167→      }\n   168→\n   169→      toast.success(`${emoji} ${type} logged!`)\n   170→      return true\n   171→    } catch (error) {\n   172→      console.error('Error logging activity:', error)\n   173→\n   174→      // Note: Don't queue activities with photos or medical data for offline sync (too complex)\n   175→      if (!photoFile && !medicalData) {\n   176→        const activity = {\n   177→          type,\n   178→          emoji,\n   179→          timestamp: Date.now(),\n   180→          user: householdStore.currentMember,\n   181→          petId: petsStore.selectedPetId === 'all' ? 203→      const activityRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities/${activityId}`)\n   204→      await remove(activityRef)\n   205→      toast.success('Activity return false\n   211→    }\n   212→  }\n   213→\n   214→  async function updateActivity(activityId, updates) {\n   215→    if (!householdStore.householdId) {\n   216→      toast.error('Please sign in 222→      await update(activityRef, updates)\n   223→      toast.success('Activity updated')\n   224→      return true\n   225→    } catch (error) {\n   226→      console.error('Error updating activity:', error)\n   227→      toast.error('Failed to update activity')\n   228→      return false\n   229→    }\n   230→  }\n   231→\n   232→  async function syncOfflineQueue() {\n   233→    if (offlineQueue.value.length === 0) return\n   234→\n   235→    const queue = [...offlineQueue.value]\n   236→    offlineQueue.value = []\n   237→\n   238→    for (const activity of queue) {\n   239→      try {\n   240→        const activitiesRef = dbRef(database, `households/${householdStore.householdId}/activities`)\n   241→        await push(activitiesRef, activity)\n   242→      } catch (error) {\n   243→        console.error('Failed to sync offline activity:', error)\n   244→        offlineQueue.value.push(activity)\n   245→      }\n   246→    }\n   247→\n   248→    if (offlineQueue.value.length === 0) {\n   249→      toast.success('All offline activities synced!')\n   250→    }\n   251→  }\n   252→\n   253→  // Load offline queue from localStorage\n   254→  function loadOfflineQueue() {\n   255→    const saved = localStorage.getItem('offlineQueue')\n   256→    if (saved) {\n   257→      try {\n   258→        offlineQueue.value = JSON.parse(saved)\n activities,\n   273→    loading,\n   274→    offlineQueue,\n   275→\n   276→    // Computed\n   277→    sortedActivities,\n   278→    todayActivities,\n   279→    stats,\n   280→\n   281→    // Now I have thoroughly examined the codebase. Let me compile the comprehensive audit report:\n\n---\n\n# COMPREHENSIVE SECURITY & CODE QUALITY AUDIT - Tailr Vue.js Application\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThe Tailr application demonstrates a solid foundation with Vue 3 Composition API patterns and proper Firebase integration. However, several security, accessibility, and code quality issues require attention before production deployment. The codebase contains **23 issues** across 5 categories ranging from critical security concerns to best practice violations.\n\n---\n\n## 1. CRITICAL SECURITY ISSUES\n\n### 1.1 Plaintext Passcode Storage in Firebase ⚠️ CRITICAL\n\n**Severity:** HIGH  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js` (line 51)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\npasscode: passcodeValue, // TODO: Hash this in production\n```\n\n**Problem:** Household passcodes are stored in plaintext in Firebase Realtime Database. This violates fundamental security principles.\n\n**Impact:**\n- Any database accessor can view household passcodes\n- No encryption or hashing applied\n- Explicit TODO comment indicates awareness of the issue\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Implement bcrypt or argon2 hashing before storage\n- Never store plaintext secrets in database\n- Add server-side validation function\n\n---\n\n### 1.2 Client-Side Passcode Verification ⚠️ CRITICAL\n\n**Severity:** HIGH  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js` (lines 124-125)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nif (data.passcode !== passcodeValue) {\n  throw new Error('Incorrect passcode')\n}\n```\n\n**Problem:** Passcode verification happens on the client-side. An attacker can:\n- Bypass verification by modifying the code\n- See the passcode transmission in network requests\n- Modify Firebase rules to pass incorrect passcodes\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Move authentication to a Cloud Function\n- Validate passcodes server-side only\n- Use proper authentication tokens instead of passcodes\n\n---\n\n### 1.3 Insecure Direct Object Reference (IDOR) Risk\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js` (entire file)\n\n**Issue:** Household data is accessed directly by household code:\n```javascript\nconst householdRef = dbRef(database, `households/${code}`)\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- No validation that the user should access this household\n- Firebase rules must enforce security (not shown in audit scope)\n- Client can attempt to access any household code\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Verify Firebase security rules are properly configured\n- Add client-side validation of household membership\n- Log all household access attempts\n\n---\n\n### 1.4 Insufficient Error Messages Exposing Data\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:** Multiple locations\n\n**Issue:** Error messages logged to console and displayed to users:\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js:108` - \"Error creating household\"\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js:169` - \"Error joining household\"\n- Multiple Firebase error logs exposed\n\n**Problem:** Console errors may expose system details, stack traces, or sensitive information to users or attackers with browser dev tools.\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Log detailed errors to backend only\n- Display generic user-friendly messages\n- Never expose error codes or stack traces to UI\n\n---\n\n## 2. XSS & USER INPUT HANDLING ISSUES\n\n### 2.1 User Input Displayed Without Sanitization\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityFeed.vue` (lines 81, 93-95)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityFeed.vue` (lines 101-126)\n\n**Issue:**\n```vue\n<h4 class=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n  {{ activity.type }}\n</h4>\n<p v-if=\"activity.notes\" class=\"text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mt-1\">\n  {{ activity.notes }}\n</p>\n```\n\n**Problem:** \n- User-provided notes field (max 200 chars) is displayed via `{{ }}` binding (safe in Vue)\n- However, medical data fields like `vaccineName`, `cost`, and custom notes are displayed without validation\n- No input validation on what's stored in Firebase\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Add input validation at form submission\n- Implement content security policy (CSP) headers\n- Consider HTML escaping for any backend-supplied data\n\n---\n\n### 2.2 Missing Input Validation on Medical Data\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/MedicalModal.vue` (lines 64, 77, 96)\n\n**Issue:**\n```vue\n<input\n  v-model.number=\"formData.cost\"\n  type=\"number\"\n  step=\"0.01\"\n  min=\"0\"\n/>\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Medical data (cost, weight, vaccine names) relies only on HTML5 input types\n- No backend validation of medical field ranges\n- Cost could theoretically be negative or excessively large (JS number limits)\n- Weight field accepts any positive number without range validation\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Add client-side range validation (cost: 0-10000, weight: 0.1-500)\n- Implement Firebase Cloud Functions for server-side validation\n- Add unit tests for boundary conditions\n\n---\n\n## 3. ERROR HANDLING & RESILIENCE ISSUES\n\n### 3.1 Missing Error Handling in Critical Paths\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/InviteMemberModal.vue` (line 243)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/composables/usePdfExport.js` (lines 301, 375)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nconsole.error('Error sending email:', error)\n// No user-facing error notification\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Some error handlers only log to console, don't notify user\n- Email invitation failures silently fail with no user feedback\n- PDF generation errors may not surface to UI properly\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Ensure all try/catch blocks emit toast notifications\n- Add retry logic for transient failures\n- Log errors to backend for monitoring\n\n---\n\n### 3.2 Unhandled Promise Rejections Possible\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js` (line 160)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/pets.js` (line 89)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nawait push(activitiesRef, activity)\n// No explicit error handling for push failures\n// Relies on parent try/catch\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Promise rejections could escape if parent try/catch is removed\n- Offline queue sync (line 241) has error handling but could be more robust\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Add explicit `.catch()` handlers in addition to try/catch\n- Add connection state monitoring\n- Implement exponential backoff for retries\n\n---\n\n### 3.3 Firebase Listener Error Handling\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js` (lines 81-84)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/pets.js` (lines 53-56)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nlistener.value = onValue(activitiesRef, (snapshot) => {\n  // success path\n}, (error) => {\n  console.error('Firebase listener error:', error)\n  toast.error('Failed to sync activities')\n})\n```\n\n**Positive:** Proper error callback provided. But consider:\n- Should reconnection be attempted automatically?\n- Should sync state be tracked in UI?\n\n---\n\n## 4. PERFORMANCE & MEMORY ISSUES\n\n### 4.1 Event Listener Memory Leak Risk in App.vue\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/App.vue` (lines 30-32)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\ndarkModeMediaQuery.addEventListener('change', (e) => {\n  isDarkMode.value = e.matches\n})\n// No removeEventListener call\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Event listener added in `onMounted` but never removed\n- While unlikely to cause issues (page rarely reloads), violates best practices\n- Memory leak potential in SPA navigation\n\n**Recommendation:**\n```javascript\nonMounted(() => {\n  const handler = (e) => { isDarkMode.value = e.matches }\n  darkModeMediaQuery.addEventListener('change', handler)\n  \n  onUnmounted(() => {\n    darkModeMediaQuery.removeEventListener('change', handler)\n  })\n})\n```\n\n---\n\n### 4.2 Large Computed Properties Without Optimization\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js` (lines 45-59)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityFeed.vue` (lines 308-330)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nconst stats = computed(() => {\n  const today = todayActivities.value\n  return {\n    poop: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Poop').length,\n    pee: today.filter(a => a.type === 'Pee').length,\n    // ... 7 more filter operations\n  }\n})\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Stats computed rebuilds all 9 filters on every activity change\n- `groupedActivities` in ActivityFeed rebuilds entire grouping on any activity change\n- No memoization or optimization\n\n**Impact:** Minimal (typically <100 activities), but scales poorly\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Use `useMemo`-like patterns or cache computed values\n- Consider lazy evaluation for rarely-used stats\n- Profile with large datasets (1000+ activities)\n\n---\n\n### 4.3 Missing Key in Dynamic Lists (Anti-pattern)\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityInsights.vue` (line 22)\n\n**Issue:**\n```vue\n<div\n  v-for=\"(insight, index) in insights\"\n  :key=\"index\"  <!-- Using index as key! -->\n  class=\"insight-card\"\n>\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Using index as key can cause rendering issues if items reorder\n- Not critical since insights array is computed/stable, but anti-pattern\n- Could cause animation/state issues if insights reorder\n\n**Recommendation:**\n```vue\n:key=\"`insight-${insight.type}`\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. ACCESSIBILITY ISSUES\n\n### 5.1 Missing Aria Labels on Icon-Only Buttons\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/HouseholdSettingsModal.vue` (line 64) - Copy button lacks aria-label\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/HouseholdSettingsModal.vue` (line 66) - No aria-label\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/InviteMemberModal.vue` (line 73) - Copy button no aria-label\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/views/DashboardView.vue` (line 16) - Settings button title only, no aria-label\n\n**Issue:**\n```vue\n<button\n  @click=\"copyCode\"\n  class=\"rounded-lg bg-gray-200...\"\n  title=\"Household Settings\"\n  <!-- Missing aria-label -->\n>\n  ⚙️\n</button>\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Screen readers won't announce button purpose\n- `title` attribute not announced reliably by screen readers\n- Violates WCAG 2.1 Level A (1.3.1 Info and Relationships)\n\n**Recommendation:**\n```vue\n<button\n  aria-label=\"Copy household code\"\n  title=\"Copy household code\"\n  @click=\"copyCode\"\n>\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5.2 Missing Alt Text on Images\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityFeed.vue` (line 131)\n\n**Issue:**\n```vue\n<img\n  v-if=\"activity.photoUrl\"\n  :src=\"activity.photoUrl\"\n  alt=\"Activity photo\"  <!-- Too generic -->\n/>\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Alt text is generic, not descriptive\n- Should include pet name and activity type\n- Context-specific alt text is better for accessibility\n\n**Recommendation:**\n```vue\n:alt=\"`${activity.type} photo for activity`\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5.3 Missing Role Attributes on Custom Widgets\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/PetSelector.vue` - Chips lack role=\"radio\" or role=\"option\"\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/MemberSelector.vue` - Chips lack accessible roles\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/WeightTrendChart.vue` - Chart canvas needs role=\"img\"\n\n**Issue:**\n```vue\n<button\n  v-for=\"pet in pets\"\n  :key=\"pet.id\"\n  @click=\"selectPet(pet.id)\"\n  class=\"pet-chip\"\n>\n  {{ pet.emoji }} {{ pet.name }}\n</button>\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Appears to be a selector widget but uses buttons\n- No semantic meaning for assistive technology\n- Should use proper ARIA attributes\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Use `role=\"radio\"` + `aria-checked` for mutually exclusive selectors\n- Or refactor as `<select>` element\n\n---\n\n## 6. BEST PRACTICE VIOLATIONS\n\n### 6.1 Browser API Usage Without Fallback\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/onboarding/HouseholdSetupStep.vue` (line 97)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nawait navigator.clipboard.writeText(props.householdCode)\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Clipboard API requires HTTPS (except localhost)\n- Will fail silently on HTTP\n- No fallback for older browsers\n- Error handling logs to console but doesn't inform user\n\n**Recommendation:**\n```javascript\ntry {\n  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(props.householdCode)\n} catch {\n  // Fallback: prompt user to copy manually\n  // Or use older method with document.execCommand\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6.2 Using confirm() for Critical Actions\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/views/DashboardView.vue` (line 328)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/HouseholdSettingsModal.vue` (line 218)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nif (confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this activity?')) {\n  await activitiesStore.deleteActivity(activityId)\n}\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Browser `confirm()` modal has poor UX\n- Cannot be customized with branding\n- Accessibility issues (especially on mobile)\n- No undo capability\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Replace with custom modal component\n- Offer undo option after deletion\n- Add confirmation code or double-click pattern for critical actions\n\n---\n\n### 6.3 Insecure Random Number Generation\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/onboarding/SuccessStep.vue` (lines 100-102)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nconst left = Math.random() * 100\nconst delay = Math.random() * 500\nconst duration = 1000 + Math.random() * 1000\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- `Math.random()` not cryptographically secure\n- Only used for animation randomization (not a real risk)\n- But establishes bad pattern if ever used for security\n\n**Note:** This is acceptable for visual effects only. Not a real security issue.\n\n---\n\n### 6.4 Exception Swallowing in JSON.parse\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js` (lines 256-262)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\ntry {\n  offlineQueue.value = JSON.parse(saved)\n} catch (error) {\n  console.error('Failed to load offline queue:', error)\n  // silently continues with empty queue\n}\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Error is logged but user doesn't know sync queue was lost\n- Could lose offline-saved activities\n- No recovery mechanism\n\n**Recommendation:**\n```javascript\n} catch (error) {\n  console.error('Failed to load offline queue:', error)\n  localStorage.removeItem('offlineQueue') // clear corrupted data\n  // Could also log to backend for monitoring\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6.5 Missing Exhaustive Dependencies Check\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/EditActivityModal.vue` (line 126)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nwatch(() => props.activity, (newActivity) => {\n  if (newActivity) {\n    const date = new Date(newActivity.timestamp)\n    // ...\n  }\n}, { immediate: true })  // ✓ Has immediate option - GOOD\n```\n\n**Note:** Most watches are properly configured. This is actually GOOD.\n\n---\n\n## 7. DATA HANDLING ISSUES\n\n### 7.1 Potential Race Condition in Offline Queue Sync\n\n**Severity:** LOW  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js` (lines 232-251)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nasync function syncOfflineQueue() {\n  if (offlineQueue.value.length === 0) return\n\n  const queue = [...offlineQueue.value]\n  offlineQueue.value = []  // Clear immediately\n\n  for (const activity of queue) {\n    try {\n      // ...push each activity\n    } catch (error) {\n      offlineQueue.value.push(activity)  // Re-queue on failure\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- If sync is called multiple times concurrently, could lose items\n- Not protected by lock/semaphore\n- User could trigger sync twice by navigating quickly\n\n**Impact:** Low probability, but potential data loss\n\n**Recommendation:**\n```javascript\nlet isSyncing = false\n\nasync function syncOfflineQueue() {\n  if (isSyncing || offlineQueue.value.length === 0) return\n  \n  try {\n    isSyncing = true\n    // ... sync logic\n  } finally {\n    isSyncing = false\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. CONSOLE LOGGING REVIEW\n\n### 8.1 Console.error Statements (23 instances)\n\n**Locations with console.error:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js` (4 instances)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js` (7 instances)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/pets.js` (3 instances)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/composables/useAnalytics.js` (3 instances)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/composables/usePdfExport.js` (2 instances)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/views/OnboardingView.vue` (2 instances)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/InviteMemberModal.vue` (1 instance)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/onboarding/HouseholdSetupStep.vue` (1 instance)\n\n**Assessment:** \n- ✓ Appropriately used for debugging\n- ✓ No sensitive data logged\n- ✗ Could be removed or replaced with proper error logging service\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Implement Sentry or similar error tracking\n- Use environment-based conditional logging\n- Remove console.error in production builds\n\n---\n\n## 9. DATA PRIVACY CONCERNS\n\n### 9.1 Household Code + Passcode Combination Risk\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Overall Issue:** While two-factor (code + passcode) is good, the combination is weak:\n- Household code can be easily guessed or brute-forced\n- 6-digit passcode has only 1,000,000 combinations\n- No rate limiting on join attempts\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Implement exponential backoff after failed join attempts\n- Add CAPTCHA after 3 failed attempts\n- Consider email-based verification instead of passcode\n\n---\n\n### 9.2 Email Exposure in Invite System\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM  \n**Files:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js` (line 244)\n\n**Issue:**\n```javascript\nconst docRef = await addDoc(mailCollection, {\n  to: recipientEmail,\n  template: {\n    data: {\n      inviterName: memberName.value,\n      householdName: householdName.value,\n    }\n  }\n})\n```\n\n**Problem:**\n- Email addresses stored in Firestore (visible in logs)\n- No rate limiting on email invites\n- Could be used for spam\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Add rate limiting (1 email per member per hour)\n- Hash email before logging\n- Implement unsubscribe mechanism\n\n---\n\n## 10. MISSING SECURITY FEATURES\n\n### 10.1 No CSRF Protection\n\n**Issue:** No explicit CSRF token handling for critical operations\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Implement Firebase custom claims for authorization\n- Use signed tokens instead of household codes\n\n---\n\n### 10.2 No Input Rate Limiting\n\n**Issue:** No protection against rapid-fire requests\n\n**Recommendation:**\n- Implement client-side throttling for activity logging\n- Server-side rate limiting in Cloud Functions\n\n---\n\n## SUMMARY TABLE\n\n| Category | Count | Severity | Status |\n|----------|-------|----------|--------|\n| Security | 4 | HIGH-MEDIUM | 🔴 |\n| XSS/Input | 2 | MEDIUM | 🔴 |\n| Error Handling | 3 | MEDIUM-LOW | 🟡 |\n| Performance | 3 | MEDIUM-LOW | 🟡 |\n| Accessibility | 3 | MEDIUM | 🔴 |\n| Best Practices | 4 | LOW-MEDIUM | 🟡 |\n| Data Handling | 2 | MEDIUM-LOW | 🟡 |\n| Privacy | 2 | MEDIUM | 🟡 |\n\n**Total Issues Found: 23**\n- Critical/High: 4\n- Medium: 10\n- Low: 9\n\n---\n\n## PRIORITY REMEDIATION ROADMAP\n\n**PHASE 1 (CRITICAL - Before Production):**\n1. Implement passcode hashing (Firebase Cloud Function)\n2. Move passcode verification to server-side Cloud Function\n3. Add Firebase Security Rules validation\n4. Implement input validation on medical data\n\n**PHASE 2 (HIGH - First Week):**\n5. Add error logging service (Sentry)\n6. Replace `confirm()` with custom modals\n7. Fix event listener cleanup in App.vue\n8. Add aria-labels to icon buttons\n\n**PHASE 3 (MEDIUM - First Month):**\n9. Implement rate limiting\n10. Add offline queue race condition protection\n11. Improve error messages (generic to users, detailed to logs)\n12. Add comprehensive error handling to all async operations\n\n**PHASE 4 (LOW - Ongoing):**\n13. Refactor selector components with proper ARIA roles\n14. Optimize computed properties\n15. Add Clipboard API fallback\n16. {"parentUuid":"8131fc7e-0a27-41bd-aef5-c18db08cb044","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"38a5de0e-3913-448e-99e7-b9ec4e56ebc3","agentId":"a9cd501724679ed01","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QpidSbcfqMp4R6hqMMQ3eA","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→import { jsPDF } from 'jspdf'\n     2→import { format } from 'date-fns'\n     3→\n     4→export function usePdfExport() {\n     5→  /**\n     6→   * Generate a medical history PDF for a pet\n     7→   * @param {Object} pet - Pet object with name, emoji, species\n     8→   * @param {Array} activities - All activities for the pet\n     9→   * @param {Object} household - Household info\n    10→   */\n    11→  function generateMedicalPdf(pet, activities, household) {\n    12→    try {\n    13→      // Create new PDF (portrait, mm, A4)\n    14→      const doc = new jsPDF('p', 'mm', 'a4')\n    15→      const pageWidth = doc.internal.pageSize.getWidth()\n    16→      const pageHeight = doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight()\n    17→      const margin = 20\n    18→      const contentWidth = pageWidth - (margin * 2)\n    19→      let yPos = margin\n    20→\n    21→      // Helper function to check if new page is needed\n    22→      const checkNewPage = (neededHeight) => {\n    23→        if (yPos + neededHeight > pageHeight - margin) {\n    24→          doc.addPage()\n    25→          yPos = margin\n    26→          return true\n    27→        }\n    28→        return false\n    29→      }\n    30→\n    31→      // Helper function to add footer\n    32→      const addFooter = () => {\n    33→        const pageNum = doc.internal.getCurrentPageInfo().pageNumber\n    34→        doc.setFontSize(8)\n    35→        doc.setTextColor(150)\n    36→        doc.text(\n    37→          `Generated by Tailr - 74→\n    75→  // Helper: Get activity counts by type\n    76→  const getCountByType = (activities, type) => {\n    77→    return activities.filter(a => a.type === type).length\n    78→  }\n    79→\n    80→  // Helper: Calculate daily average for a type\n    81→  const getDailyAverage = (activities, type) => {\n    82→    const count = getCountByType(activities, type)\n    83→    const days = v-if=\"weightData.length === 0\" class=\"empty-state\">\n    24→      <p class=\"empty-icon\">📊</p>\n    25→      <p class=\"empty-text\">No weight data yet</p>\n    26→      <p class=\"empty-hint\">Log weight checks to see trends over time</p>\n    27→    </div>\n    28→\n    29→    <div v-else class=\"chart-container\">\n    30→      <canvas ref=\"chartCanvas\"></canvas>\n    31→      <div class=\"chart-stats\">\n    32→        <div class=\"stat-item\">\n    33→          <span class=\"stat-label\">Latest</span>\n    34→          <span class=\"stat-value\">{{ latestWeight }} {{ displayUnit }}</span>\n    35→        </div>\n    36→        <div class=\"stat-item\" v-if=\"weightChange !== null\">\n    37→          <span class=\"stat-label\">Change</span>\n    38→          <span\n    39→            class=\"stat-value\"\n    40→            :class=\"{\n    41→              'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400': weightChange > 0,\n    42→              'text-green-600 Now I have a comprehensive view of the codebase. Let me compile my security and code quality audit report.\n\n---\n\n# TAILR VUE.JS APPLICATION - SECURITY & CODE QUALITY AUDIT REPORT\n\n## EXECUTIVE SUMMARY\n\nThe Tailr application demonstrates **solid architecture** with proper use of Vue 3 composition API, Pinia stores, and Firebase integration. However, several **moderate to high-priority security, accessibility, and code quality issues** have been identified that should be addressed before production deployment.\n\n---\n\n## 1. SECURITY ISSUES\n\n### 1.1 CRITICAL: Plaintext Passcode Storage in Firebase\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js`\n**Lines:** 51, 75, 124, 148\n\n**Issue:** Passcodes are stored in plaintext in Firebase Realtime Database. This is a severe security vulnerability.\n\n```javascript\n// Line 51 - plaintext passcode written to database\npasscode: passcodeValue, // TODO: Hash this in production\n\n// Lines 75, 148 - stored in local variable but still exposed\npasscode.value = passcodeValue\n```\n\n**Risk:** Any user who gains database access can view all household passcodes. This violates basic password security principles.\n\n**Recommended Fix:**\n- Never store plain-text passwords/passcodes in any database\n- Implement server-side password hashing (bcrypt, Argon2) via Firebase Functions\n- Verify passcode only during join operation, never store it\n\n---\n\n### 1.2 HIGH: Event Listener Memory Leak\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/App.vue`\n**Lines:** 26-32\n\n**Issue:** Media query listener is added but never removed when the component unmounts, causing a memory leak.\n\n```javascript\nonMounted(() => {\n  const darkModeMediaQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)')\n  \n  // Listener added but never removed\n  darkModeMediaQuery.addEventListener('change', (e) => {\n    isDarkMode.value = e.matches\n  })\n  // Missing onUnmounted(() => darkModeMediaQuery.removeEventListener(...))\n})\n```\n\n**Risk:** Memory leak on page navigation; event listeners accumulate over time in SPAs.\n\n**Recommended Fix:** Add cleanup in `onUnmounted` hook.\n\n---\n\n### 1.3 HIGH: Unhandled Promise Rejections in Composables\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/composables/useAnalytics.js`\n**Lines:** 17-21, 104-108, 118-122\n\n**Issue:** While error handling exists, no validation that `analytics` object is properly initialized before use.\n\n```javascript\nconst trackEvent = (eventName, eventParams = {}) => {\n  if (!analytics) return // Silently fails\n  try {\n    logEvent(analytics, eventName, eventParams)\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Analytics tracking error:', error) // Only logs to console\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** Errors are silently logged to console; users won't be notified of tracking failures in production.\n\n---\n\n### 1.4 MEDIUM: Clipboard API Error Not Handled\n\n**Files:** \n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/InviteMemberModal.vue` (Lines 196, 206)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/HouseholdSettingsModal.vue` (Line 205)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/onboarding/HouseholdSetupStep.vue` (Line 97)\n\n**Issue:** `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` calls are not wrapped in try-catch blocks.\n\n```javascript\nfunction copyLink() {\n  navigator.clipboard.writeText(inviteLink.value)\n  // No error handling for clipboard access denial\n  linkCopied.value = true\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** Silent failures if user denies clipboard access; no user feedback on failure.\n\n**Recommended Fix:** Wrap all clipboard operations in try-catch blocks.\n\n---\n\n### 1.5 MEDIUM: Native Alert/Confirm Used Instead of Custom UI\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityNotesModal.vue`\n**Lines:** 138, 144, 158\n\n**Issue:** Using browser `alert()` and `confirm()` instead of custom toast/modal system.\n\n```javascript\nif (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {\n  alert('Please select an image file') // Native browser alert\n}\n\nif (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {\n  alert('Photo must be less than 5MB') // Native browser alert\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** \n- Inconsistent UX (native alerts look different)\n- Cannot be styled to match app theme\n- Poor accessibility on some devices\n\n**Recommended Fix:** Use the existing `useToast()` composable instead.\n\n---\n\n### 1.6 MEDIUM: File Upload Validation Missing MIME Type Verification\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityNotesModal.vue`\n**Lines:** 136-147\n\n**Issue:** File type validation only checks `file.type` which can be spoofed by malicious users.\n\n```javascript\nif (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {\n  alert('Please select an image file')\n  return\n}\n// No additional validation (magic number checking, file extension verification)\n```\n\n**Risk:** Malicious users could upload renamed executable files; server-side validation is missing.\n\n**Recommended Fix:** \n- Add server-side file validation in Firebase Storage rules\n- Implement magic number (file signature) verification\n- Verify file extensions whitelist\n\n---\n\n### 1.7 MEDIUM: Sensitive Data in LocalStorage Without Expiration\n\n**File:** Multiple files use localStorage:\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/pets.js` (Line 16)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js` (Lines 12-14, 21)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js` (Line 255-267)\n\n**Issue:** Household credentials stored indefinitely in localStorage without expiration.\n\n```javascript\nconst householdId = ref(localStorage.getItem('householdId') || null)\nconst householdCode = ref(localStorage.getItem('householdCode') || null)\nconst memberName = ref(localStorage.getItem('memberName') || '')\n// No expiration; credentials persist even after browser close\n```\n\n**Risk:** \n- Stolen device = stolen household credentials\n- No session timeout\n- Offline queue data persisted insecurely\n\n**Recommended Fix:**\n- Use sessionStorage for temporary credentials (cleared on browser close)\n- Implement session expiration with re-authentication\n- Add data encryption in localStorage\n\n---\n\n### 1.8 MEDIUM: No XSS Protection Verified\n\n**Files:** Activity feed and medical data display\n\n**Note:** While no `v-html` or `innerHTML` usage found, user-input data (notes, medical data) is directly interpolated:\n\n```javascript\n// ActivityFeed.vue, Line 94\n<p v-if=\"activity.notes\" class=\"text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 mt-1\">\n  {{ activity.notes }} <!-- Direct interpolation, safe from XSS -->\n</p>\n```\n\n**Status:** ✅ Properly mitigated (Vue auto-escapes by default), but documentation recommendation: always verify when adding new text interpolations.\n\n---\n\n## 2. ERROR HANDLING ISSUES\n\n### 2.1 HIGH: Missing Error Boundaries for Critical Operations\n\n**Files:** \n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/views/DashboardView.vue` (Lines 287-296)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/views/OnboardingView.vue` (Lines 185-212)\n\n**Issue:** Listeners are started without try-catch, but errors are only caught on the listener callback.\n\n```javascript\nonMounted(() => {\n  activitiesStore.startListener() // No try-catch\n  petsStore.startListener() // No try-catch\n  \n  activitiesStore.loadOfflineQueue()\n  // If loadOfflineQueue fails, app state is corrupted\n})\n```\n\n**Risk:** If listeners fail to initialize, user sees incomplete UI without error notification.\n\n---\n\n### 2.2 MEDIUM: Offline Queue Persistence Not Validated\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js`\n**Lines:** 254-262\n\n**Issue:** Reading from localStorage without validation that JSON is parseable.\n\n```javascript\nfunction loadOfflineQueue() {\n  const saved = localStorage.getItem('offlineQueue')\n  if (saved) {\n    try {\n      offlineQueue.value = JSON.parse(saved)\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Failed to load offline queue:', error)\n      // queue remains in broken state, user doesn't know\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** Corrupted offline queue silently ignored; users lose offline activities.\n\n**Recommended Fix:** \n- Clear broken queue\n- Show toast notification\n- Initialize to empty array\n\n---\n\n### 2.3 MEDIUM: No Retry Logic for Failed Activity Sync\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js`\n**Lines:** 232-250\n\n**Issue:** Offline queue sync has basic retry but no exponential backoff or max retry limit.\n\n```javascript\nasync function syncOfflineQueue() {\n  for (const activity of queue) {\n    try {\n      await push(activitiesRef, activity)\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Failed to sync offline activity:', error)\n      offlineQueue.value.push(activity) // Re-adds to queue without retry limit\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** Failed activities can be retried indefinitely, consuming user data/battery.\n\n---\n\n## 3. PERFORMANCE ISSUES\n\n### 3.1 HIGH: Expensive Computed Property in ActivityInsights\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityInsights.vue`\n**Lines:** 55-241\n\n**Issue:** Large computed property recalculates for every activity array change, with 8+ complex calculations.\n\n```javascript\nconst insights = computed(() => {\n  const results = []\n  const now = Date.now() // Recalculates on EVERY activity update\n  // ... 186 lines of complex date/math operations ...\n  return results.sort((a, b) => ...)\n})\n```\n\n**Risk:** \n- Noticeable lag when logging new activities\n- Jank on low-end devices\n- Recalculates even for unrelated prop changes\n\n**Recommended Fix:**\n- Memoize with `useMemo` or computed with dependency optimization\n- Move expensive calculations to utility functions\n- Debounce activity updates\n\n---\n\n### 3.2 MEDIUM: Full Array Sort on Every Render\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/activities.js`\n**Lines:** 33-35\n\n**Issue:** `sortedActivities` creates new array copy and sorts on every dependency change.\n\n```javascript\nconst sortedActivities = computed(() => {\n  return [...filteredActivities.value].sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)\n  // O(n log n) on every change\n})\n```\n\n**Risk:** Large activity lists (>1000 items) will cause perceptible lag.\n\n**Recommended Fix:** Sort activities once in Firebase listener, maintain sorted order.\n\n---\n\n### 3.3 MEDIUM: Memory Leak in Chart Component\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/WeightTrendChart.vue`\n**Lines:** 144-150\n\n**Issue:** Previous chart instance is destroyed but canvas reference may not be properly cleaned.\n\n```javascript\nfunction createChart() {\n  if (!chartCanvas.value || chartData.value.length === 0) return\n  \n  if (chartInstance.value) {\n    chartInstance.value.destroy()\n    // chartInstance.value not set to null\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** Multiple chart instances may remain in memory if updates are frequent.\n\n---\n\n## 4. ACCESSIBILITY ISSUES\n\n### 4.1 MEDIUM: Missing ARIA Labels on Icon Buttons\n\n**Files:**\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityFeed.vue` (Lines 145-156)\n- `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ToastContainer.vue` (Line 12-17)\n\n**Issue:** Buttons with emoji icons lack `aria-label` or `title` attributes for screen readers.\n\n```vue\n<!-- ToastContainer.vue, Line 12-17 -->\n<button\n  @click=\"remove(toast.id)\"\n  class=\"text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600\"\n  <!-- ✕ button missing aria-label -->\n>\n  ✕\n</button>\n\n<!-- Correct: Has aria-label -->\n<!-- ActivityFeed.vue, Line 145-147 -->\n<button\n  @click=\"handleEdit(activity)\"\n  title=\"Edit activity\"\n  aria-label=\"Edit activity\" <!-- ✓ Has aria-label -->\n>\n  ✏️\n</button>\n```\n\n**Risk:** Screen reader users can't understand button purpose.\n\n**Recommended Fix:** Add `aria-label` to all icon buttons consistently.\n\n---\n\n### 4.2 MEDIUM: Color-Only Information Conveyance\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityInsights.vue`\n**Lines:** 348-373\n\n**Issue:** Insights cards use color to indicate severity but no text label or icon.\n\n```css\n.insight-card.insight-alert {\n  border-left-color: #ef4444; /* Red indicates alert */\n  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.05);\n  /* No accompanying text or icon */\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** Color-blind users can't distinguish alert levels.\n\n**Recommended Fix:** Add text labels (\"Alert\", \"Info\", \"Warning\") in addition to color.\n\n---\n\n### 4.3 MEDIUM: Image Missing Alt Text (Edge Case)\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityFeed.vue`\n**Lines:** 130-136\n\n**Status:** ✅ Correctly implemented\n\n```vue\n<img\n  v-if=\"activity.photoUrl\"\n  :src=\"activity.photoUrl\"\n  alt=\"Activity photo\"  <!-- ✓ Has alt text -->\n  class=\"mt-2 rounded-lg max-w-full h-auto\"\n/>\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. VUE & JAVASCRIPT BEST PRACTICES\n\n### 5.1 MEDIUM: Unsafe Array Key in v-for\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityInsights.vue`\n**Lines:** 20-34\n\n**Issue:** Using array index as key in v-for loop with dynamic arrays.\n\n```vue\n<div\n  v-for=\"(insight, index) in insights\"\n  :key=\"index\"  <!-- ✗ Using index as key - BAD -->\n  class=\"insight-card\"\n>\n  {{ insight.message }}\n</div>\n```\n\n**Risk:** \n- Reordering/filtering insights breaks component state\n- Animation/transitions fail\n- Input state persists incorrectly\n\n**Recommended Fix:** Create unique IDs for insights or use message content as key.\n\n---\n\n### 5.2 MEDIUM: Prop Validation Missing on StatsWidget\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/StatsWidget.vue`\n**Lines:** 59-77\n\n**Issue:** Stats object has default but all properties lack type validation.\n\n```javascript\ndefineProps({\n  stats: {\n    type: Object,\n    required: true,\n    default: () => ({  // ✗ Default provided for required prop (contradictory)\n      poop: 0,\n      pee: 0,\n      food: 0,\n      // ... no individual property validation\n    })\n  }\n})\n```\n\n**Risk:** Runtime errors if required properties missing; no compile-time type checking.\n\n---\n\n### 5.3 MEDIUM: Missing Boundary Case Handling\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/composables/useAnalytics.js`\n**Lines:** 14-22\n\n**Issue:** No check if `logEvent` is undefined or if analytics initialization failed.\n\n```javascript\nconst trackEvent = (eventName, eventParams = {}) => {\n  if (!analytics) return\n  \n  try {\n    logEvent(analytics, eventName, eventParams) // What if logEvent undefined?\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Analytics tracking error:', error)\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. DATA VALIDATION ISSUES\n\n### 6.1 HIGH: No Input Sanitization for Household Code\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js`\n**Lines:** 49, 115\n\n**Issue:** Household codes are not validated before database write.\n\n```javascript\nasync function createHousehold(code, passcodeValue, firstMemberName) {\n  try {\n    const newHouseholdRef = dbRef(database, `households/${code}`)\n    // No validation of 'code' parameter\n    // Could contain slashes, null bytes, or invalid characters\n    const snapshot = await get(newHouseholdRef)\n```\n\n**Risk:** \n- Path traversal attacks\n- Invalid database paths\n- Naming collisions\n\n**Recommended Fix:**\n```javascript\nif (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9-]{4,20}$/.test(code)) {\n  throw new Error('Invalid household code format')\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6.2 MEDIUM: No Validation on Member Names\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js`\n**Lines:** 55-67\n\n**Issue:** Member names accepted without validation.\n\n```javascript\n[firstMemberName]: { // No validation of name\n  name: firstMemberName,\n  role: 'owner',\n  // ...\n}\n```\n\n**Risk:** Extremely long names, special characters, or null values crash UI.\n\n---\n\n### 6.3 MEDIUM: Unchecked Array Access\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityInsights.vue`\n**Lines:** 159-160\n\n**Issue:** Direct array access without bounds checking.\n\n```javascript\nconst firstWeight = weightChecks[0]\nconst lastWeight = weightChecks[weightChecks.length - 1]\n// What if weightChecks has only 1 element or is empty?\n```\n\n**Risk:** Logic errors when edge cases not handled.\n\n---\n\n## 7. BEST PRACTICES VIOLATIONS\n\n### 7.1 MEDIUM: Inconsistent Error Toast Messages\n\n**Files:** Multiple stores and components\n\n**Issue:** Some errors show user-facing messages, others just console.log.\n\n```javascript\n// Activities store - user notified\ntoast.error('Failed to log activity')\n\n// Household store - only console logged\nconsole.error('Error creating household:', error)\nthrow error // Let parent component handle\n```\n\n**Risk:** Inconsistent UX; user confusion about failed operations.\n\n**Recommended:** Standardize on consistent error handling pattern.\n\n---\n\n### 7.2 MEDIUM: Magic Numbers Without Constants\n\n**File:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/components/ActivityFeed.vue`\n**Lines:** 196-197\n\n**Issue:** Hardcoded swipe thresholds without explanation.\n\n```javascript\nconst SWIPE_THRESHOLD = -80 // Swipe distance to trigger delete reveal\nconst SWIPE_DELETE_THRESHOLD = -120 // Swipe distance to auto-delete\n\n// But also:\nstate.transform = Math.max(deltaX, -150) // ← Magic number -150\n```\n\n**Risk:** Inconsistent behavior; hard to maintain.\n\n---\n\n### 7.3 LOW: Console Statements Left in Production Code\n\n**Files:** Multiple stores\n**Count:** 21 console.error/log statements\n\n**Example:** `/home/user/Pet-App/src/stores/household.js` Line 108\n```javascript\nconsole.error('Error creating household:', error)\n```\n\n**Issue:** Console statements should be removed or converted to debug logging.\n\n**Risk:** \n- Exposes internal errors to users\n- Noise in production logs\n- Potential information disclosure\n\n---\n\n## 8. SECURITY BEST PRACTICES CHECKLIST\n\n| Item | Status | Location |\n|------|--------|----------|\n| Plaintext passwords in database | ❌ CRITICAL | household.js:51 |\n| HTTPS enforced | ⓘ Unknown | - |\n| CORS configured | ⓘ Unknown | - |\n| Rate limiting on API | ⓘ Unknown | - |\n| Input validation | ❌ WEAK | Multiple |\n| Output encoding | ✅ Good | Automatic via Vue |\n| Authentication timeout | ❌ None | - |\n| Secure session storage | ❌ No | Using localStorage |\n| CSRF protection | ⓘ Firebase handles | - |\n| Data encryption at rest | ⓘ Unknown | Firebase config |\n| Data encryption in transit | ✅ HTTPS assumed | - |\n| Error message disclosure | ❌ Verbose | Stores expose details |\n| Dependency audits | ⓘ Unknown | npm audit needed |\n| CSP headers | ⓘ Unknown | - |\n\n---\n\n## 9.
